# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-29)

## Corpus Check
- 4802 files · ~10,888,119 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 112 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 34, .css 17, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 67676 nodes · 190993 edges · 1733 communities (1229 shown, 504 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8101 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `81ab6eba`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
